    SELECT TOP(1) [e].[FormId], [e].[CurrencyId], [e].[EmployeeId], [e].[LastUpdatedDate], [e].[ManagerId], [e].[RejectionReason], [e].[Status], [e].[SubmissionDate], [e].[TotalAmount], [a].[Id], [a].[AccessFailedCount], [a].[ConcurrencyStamp], [a].[CreatedDate], [a].[Email], [a].[EmailConfirmed], [a].[FullName], [a].[IsActive], [a].[LockoutEnabled], [a].[LockoutEnd], [a].[ManagerId] AS [ManagerId0], [a].[NormalizedEmail], [a].[NormalizedUserName], [a].[PasswordHash], [a].[PhoneNumber], [a].[PhoneNumberConfirmed], [a].[RoleId], [a].[SecurityStamp], [a].[TwoFactorEnabled], [a].[UserName]
